Skip to main content

Community CRM

Community relationships, memberships, donations and follow-up

Login / Settings

This coursework demo has three simulated roles. All use passcode demo123. This is not secure authentication.

Application settings and data management

Records are saved in this browser's localStorage. Export a JSON backup before resetting sample data.

A production version would require server-side authentication, role enforcement, a shared database, and secure backups. Do not enter real sensitive information.

Dashboard

At-a-glance community relationship overview.

Recent activity

Upcoming / open tasks

Contacts

Create, search, update, archive, and restore people records.

Add contact

Contact Details

Select a contact to see the full profile and connected records.

Memberships

Track plans, status, start dates and renewals.

Add membership

Plans: One person $35, Family $40, Student or senior $30. Only Admin can change the plan price list. Existing payments keep their original price.

Admin: Plan prices

Donations

Record gifts and review donation history.

Add donation

Tasks

Assign follow-ups, track due dates, and mark work complete.

Add task

Reports

Summary metrics and exportable data for coursework demonstrations.

Donations by fund

Membership status

Data export

Download records for analysis or keep a JSON backup.

Login / Settings

Current demo session, role description, and data controls.

Session

Admin: all demo controls. Staff: create and update records. Viewer: read-only. These permissions are UI-only and can be bypassed; never use this as production security.

Data management

Export before resetting. Reset replaces the current local browser data with the sample dataset.

Storage key: communityCRM.v2 in this browser's localStorage. Data does not sync across devices.

history is retained.')} function active(type){return db[type].filter(r=>!r.archived)} function filtered(type,prefix){const q=document.getElementById(prefix+'-search').value.trim().toLowerCase(),filter=document.getElementById(prefix+'-filter').value;return db[type].filter(r=>(filter==='all'||(filter==='archived'?r.archived:!r.archived))&&Object.entries(r).map(([k,v])=>k==='contactId'?contactName(v):k==='householdId'?householdName(v):v).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))} function val(k,v){if(k==='contactId')return contactName(v);if(k==='householdId')return householdName(v);if(k==='emailPermission')return v?'Allowed':'Not allowed';if(k==='amount')return '$'+Number(v||0).toFixed(2);return v??''} function table(type,rows,container){const cols=defs[type].cols.filter(c=>c!=='archived');if(!rows.length){document.getElementById(container).innerHTML='

'+(row?'':'')+'
';form.onsubmit=e=>submitRecord(e,type);form.querySelector('[data-cancel]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{editing[type]=null;renderForm(type);notify('Edit cancelled.')})} function submitRecord(e,type){e.preventDefault();if(!canWrite()){notify('This account is read-only.',true);return}const data=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.target));const old=(db[type]||[]).find(x=>x.id===editing[type]);if(type==='contacts'){data.firstName=data.firstName.trim();data.lastName=data.lastName.trim();data.email=data.email.trim().toLowerCase();data.emailPermission=data.emailPermission==='true';if(!data.firstName||!data.lastName){notify('First and last name are required.',true);return}if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(data.email)){notify('Enter a valid email address.',true);return}if(db.contacts.some(c=>c.email.toLowerCase()===data.email&&c.id!==editing.contacts)){notify('That email already belongs to another contact.',true);return}}if(type==='memberships'&&data.renewalDatex.id===rid?{...x,...data,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}:x);log('EDIT',type,rid,'Updated record')}else{db[type].push({id:rid,...data,archived:false,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()});log('CREATE',type,rid,'Created record')}editing[type]=null;if(persist()){renderForm(type);notify('Saved successfully.')}} function archive(type,rid,restore){if(!canWrite())return;const r=db[type].find(x=>x.id===rid);if(!r)return;if(!confirm((restore?'Restore':'Archive')+' this record?'))return;r.archived=!restore;r.updatedAt=new Date().toISOString();log(restore?'RESTORE':'ARCHIVE',type,rid,(restore?'Restored':'Archived')+' record');if(persist())notify(restore?'Record restored.':'Record archived; history is retained.')} function active(type){return db[type].filter(r=>!r.archived)} function filtered(type,prefix){const q=document.getElementById(prefix+'-search').value.trim().toLowerCase(),filter=document.getElementById(prefix+'-filter').value;return db[type].filter(r=>(filter==='all'||(filter==='archived'?r.archived:!r.archived))&&Object.entries(r).map(([k,v])=>k==='contactId'?contactName(v):k==='householdId'?householdName(v):v).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))} function val(k,v){if(k==='contactId')return contactName(v);if(k==='householdId')return householdName(v);if(k==='emailPermission')return v?'Allowed':'Not allowed';if(k==='amount')return '$'+Number(v||0).toFixed(2);return v??''} function table(type,rows,container){const cols=defs[type].cols.filter(c=>c!=='archived');if(!rows.length){document.getElementById(container).innerHTML='

No records match this view.

';return}document.getElementById(container).innerHTML=''+cols.map(c=>'').join('')+''+rows.map(r=>''+cols.map(c=>'').join('')+'').join('')+'

go(page){currentPage=page;document.querySelectorAll('[data-page][role="tab"]').forEach(b=>{const
